实验详情（镜像 / 环境 / 启动参数 / 关键日志）

每个实验一张卡片；大日志已截断展示，「打开原文件」可跳转源目录核对。默认展开实验简介，其余点击展开。
成功TP1xPCP8xDP1_PD双机_ASYNC_AB_基线_385输出_20261103（序号168-173 ★当前最好基线）2026-11-03 12:52↑ 返回详情目录
镜像：vllm-ascend v0.30.0-a5 daily(dev166 / 20261018)
组网：双机PD分离 P=.111 8卡PCP8+dspark(kv_producer) + D=.112 DP4×TP1 dspark5图模式(enforce_eager:false) + proxy26180
输入/输出：20,000 tok × 并发 1/4/8/16/32/64 ／ 请求数 4/16/32/64/128/256 ／ 输出 385 tok（并发×4 口径）
Prefix Cache：48.5% / 57.8% / 53.1% / 55.4% / 54.3% / 54.9%（P端 /metrics 实抓，六档逐档）
TTFT：635 / 790 / 1,157 / 1,716 / 5,479 / 13,797 ms avg（max 1,067/1,616/3,042/4,600/9,748/18,428）
TPOT：17.8 / 19.3 / 22.7 / 25.4 / 25.9 / 25.5 ms（dspark块延迟；per-token ≈ /5.8，C1≈3.1ms）
吞吐（合计）：—（见 logs 六档 wall）
备注（口径与说明）：1103 中午修复退化形态后的干净基线：NFS d8_112.sh 曾被 1101 老版 .bak 覆盖丢失 eager:false 优化（TPOT +5~10ms），已单变量修复（时间戳副本 d8_112_1103_1708_eagerFalse_BASE.sh）。vs 150-155(1102晚)：C1 820→635(-23%)、C64 17267→13797(-20%)，150-155 系跨天漂移，结论数据由本批取代。全档 0 失败。与 ASYNC 批(162-167)同环境背靠背，唯一变量=ASYNC env。
实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
实验目的: 1103 中午形态修复后的 PD 双机基线六档（当前最好基线，168-173，带星标）。本目录 = 基线侧。
日期: 2026-11-03 12:52 | P=141.61.45.111 TP1xPCP8xDP1(26151) / D=141.61.45.112 DP4xTP1(26152) / MooncakeConnectorV2 / proxy 26180
变量改动: 无新增变量——与 ASYNC 批(162-167)同环境背靠背(间隔约25分钟)，唯 ASYNC 批双端多 export VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1；D 端 dspark5 图模式(enforce_eager:false) 与 1102 TPOT 优化形态一致。
预期效果: 修复退化形态后的干净基线（对照 1102 晚 150-155 判定跨天漂移幅度）。
结果: C1 635/1067ms TTFT avg/max / TPOT 17.8; C4 790/1616/19.3; C8 1157/3042/22.7; C16 1716/4600/25.4; C32 5479/9748/25.9; C64 13797/18428/25.5
  hit 六档 = 48.5/57.8/53.1/55.4/54.3/54.9% (P端/metrics实抓); 全档 0 失败。
  vs 150-155(1102晚): C1 820→635 (-23%)、C64 17267→13797 (-20%) —— 150-155 确系跨天漂移偏慢。
压测口径: 20K 输入(混50构造)/385 输出/并发×4 呼叫/温度0/流式; 请求数=并发×4 (agents.md 五)

配置差异 param_diff.mdcmd/param_diff.md打开原文件 ↗
# param_diff：基线批(168-173)
## 与 ASYNC 批(162-167)的差异
- 仅少一个 env：基线批无 VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER（ASYNC 批双端 export =1），其余完全一致（同环境背靠背，间隔约25分钟）
- 形态修复记录见 ASYNC 批目录 cmd/param_diff.md（d8_112.sh .bak 覆盖事故 + eager:false 单变量修复）
## 基线结果
- C1 635/1067/17.8 → C64 13797/18428/25.5（TTFT avg/max/TPOT），hit 48.5~57.8%，全档 0 失败
- vs 150-155(1102晚): C1 -23% / C64 -20%（150-155 系跨天漂移偏慢，结论数据由本批取代）
- 本批为当前最好基线（汇总表 168-173，带星标）
成功TP1xPCP8xDP1_PD双机_ASYNC_AB_ASYNCenv_385输出_20261103（序号162-167）2026-11-03 12:35↑ 返回详情目录
镜像：vllm-ascend v0.30.0-a5 daily(dev166 / 20261018)
组网：同基线批(168-173) + 双端 VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1（唯一变量）
输入/输出：20,000 tok × 并发 1/4/8/16/32/64 ／ 请求数 4/16/32/64/128/256 ／ 输出 385 tok（并发×4 口径）
Prefix Cache：48.5% / 57.8% / 53.1% / 55.4% / 54.3% / 54.9%（与基线逐档一致）
TTFT：652 / 806 / 1,124 / 1,924 / 5,655 / 13,914 ms avg（max 1,114/1,494/3,143/4,849/10,095/18,700）
TPOT：17.6 / 19.6 / 23.1 / 24.6 / 25.7 / 26.0 ms
吞吐（合计）：—（见 logs 六档 wall）
备注（口径与说明）：VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1（评审建议的传输层开关）同环境严格 A/B：ΔTTFT = +2.7%/+2.0%/-2.9%/+12.1%/+3.2%/+0.8% 全部噪声内（C16 反劣化），TPOT 持平。定论：本形态无正向收益。勘误：首轮 ladder_async.log 在退化形态（d8_112.sh 被 1101 老版 .bak 覆盖、draft eager:true）跑，已作废留档；跨天基线 150-155 漂移不可作 A/B 对照。
实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
实验目的: VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1（传输层异步重叠开关）在 PCP8+D 双机形态下是否有正向收益（TTFT/TPOT）。本目录 = ASYNC 侧(162-167)。
日期: 2026-11-03 12:35 | P=141.61.45.111 TP1xPCP8xDP1(26151) / D=141.61.45.112 DP4xTP1(26152) / MooncakeConnectorV2 / proxy 26180
变量改动: 双端 export VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1（唯一变量）；其余与基线批完全一致。
预期效果: D 端 decode 与 KV 传输异步重叠，TTFT 压缩。
结果: TTFT avg 652/806/1124/1924/5655/13914 vs 基线 635/790/1157/1716/5479/13797
  ΔTTFT = +2.7%/+2.0%/-2.9%/+12.1%/+3.2%/+0.8% —— 全部噪声内（C16 反劣化 +12%）
  TPOT 17.6-26.0 vs 17.8-25.9 持平; 全档 0 失败; hit 六档逐档一致。
  定论: ASYNC_TRANSFER 在本形态无正向收益。

# param_diff：ASYNC 批(162-167) vs 基线批(168-173)
## 唯一变量
- 双端 export VLLM_MOONCAKE_ASYNC_TRANSFER=1（ASYNC 批）；基线批无该 env
## 形态修复记录（本实验前置勘误，重要）
- NFS d8_112.sh 及 .bak 曾为 1101 老版(draft enforce_eager:true)，1102 的 eager:false 被覆盖丢失
  → 1103 上午 ASYNC 首轮(ladder_async.log)在退化形态跑(TPOT +5~10ms)，该轮作废
- 修复: 单变量恢复 eager:false，时间戳副本 d8_112_1103_1708_eagerFalse_BASE.sh / p8dsp_1103_1708_BASE.sh
- 教训: A/B 必须同环境背靠背，跨天数据只作参考
## 结果对比（TTFT avg ms / TPOT ms / hit%）
C1   基线 635/17.8/48.5  vs ASYNC 652/17.6/48.5  Δ+2.7%
C4   基线 790/19.3/57.8  vs ASYNC 806/19.6/57.8  Δ+2.0%
C8   基线 1157/22.7/53.1 vs ASYNC 1124/23.1/53.1 Δ-2.9%
C16  基线 1716/25.4/55.4 vs ASYNC 1924/24.6/55.4 Δ+12.1%
C32  基线 5479/25.9/54.3 vs ASYNC 5655/25.7/54.3 Δ+3.2%
C64  基线 13797/25.5/54.9 vs ASYNC 13914/26.0/54.9 Δ+0.8%
定论: ASYNC_TRANSFER=1 在本形态无正向收益（全部噪声内）。

#!/bin/bash
# exp2/3 P端: TP1xPCP4xDP2+EP producer (P=.23 producer / D=.22 consumer, MooncakeConnectorV2, 治理补丁: recompute)
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER='1'
export OMP_PROC_BIND='false'
export OMP_NUM_THREADS='10'
export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF='expandable_segments:True'
export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE='1'
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE='CCU_SCHED'
export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT='204'
export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'
export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES='0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7'
export HCCL_IF_IP='141.61.33.23'
export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
unset http_proxy https_proxy ftp_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY FTP_PROXY
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
export VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 --host 141.61.33.23 --port 26101 \
  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \
  --data-parallel-size 2 --tensor-parallel-size 1 --pipeline-parallel-size 1 \
  --data-parallel-rpc-port 19000 --prefill-context-parallel-size 4 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --block-size 32 --enforce-eager \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --enable-prefix-caching \
  --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  --trust-remote-code --kv-cache-dtype fp8 \
  --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 \
  --tool-call-parser deepseek_v4 --enable-auto-tool-choice --reasoning-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --additional-config '{"enable_cpu_binding":true,"enable_fused_mc2":0,"multistream_overlap_shared_expert":true,"enable_shared_expert_dp":true}' \
  --kv-transfer-config '{"kv_connector":"MooncakeConnectorV2","kv_role":"kv_producer","kv_port":"30000","engine_id":"0","kv_load_failure_policy":"recompute","kv_connector_extra_config":{"use_ascend_direct":true,"prefill":{"dp_size":2,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1},"decode":{"dp_size":4,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1}}}' \
  > /mnt/share/.cache/.nx26/night_1029/p_pcp4_26101.log 2>&1 &
wait

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PD 385六档(经proxy) + P端metrics窗口"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26180"
PMET = "http://127.0.0.1:26151"
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(77000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(PMET + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = last = cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if cks == 1: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
                    last = time.time()
        tpot = (last - t0 - ttft / 1000.0) / (cks - 1) * 1000 if cks > 1 else 0.0
        res.append((ttft, tpot))
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def stats(res):
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} n={len(ok)} tpot_avg={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f}"
def win(conc, per):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
print("smoke...", flush=True)
w = []; win(1, 1)
print("smoke:", stats(w), flush=True)
print("mix50 x4:", flush=True)
h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
for i in range(4):
    res, rate, wall = win(4, 1)
    print(f" wave{i}: {stats(res)} hit_cum={rate:.1f}%", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
print(f"MIX50_WINDOW rate={100.0*dh/dq if dq else 0:.1f}%", flush=True)
for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
    res, rate, wall = win(conc, 4)
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    if ok:
        tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
        print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} tpot={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
    time.sleep(3)
print("PD-LADDER-DONE", flush=True)

#!/bin/bash
# PD-P: TP1xPCP4xDP1 卡4-7 dspark5同挂 prefix on kv_producer (:26151)

export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE='1'
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE='CCU_SCHED'
export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT='204'
export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'
export VLLM_ENGINE_READY_TIMEOUT_S='7200'
export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'

vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 --host 0.0.0.0 --port 26151 \
  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \
  --data-parallel-size 1 --tensor-parallel-size 1 --pipeline-parallel-size 1 \
  --data-parallel-rpc-port 19001 --prefill-context-parallel-size 4 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --speculative-config '{"method":"dspark","model":"/mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-DSpark","num_speculative_tokens":5,"enforce_eager":true}' \
  --block-size 32 --enforce-eager \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --enable-prefix-caching --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  --trust-remote-code --kv-cache-dtype fp8 \
  --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 \
  --tool-call-parser deepseek_v4 --enable-auto-tool-choice --reasoning-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --additional-config '{"enable_cpu_binding":true,"enable_fused_mc2":0,"multistream_overlap_shared_expert":true,"enable_shared_expert_dp":true}' \
  --kv-transfer-config '{"kv_connector":"MooncakeConnectorV2","kv_role":"kv_producer","kv_port":"30000","engine_id":"0","kv_load_failure_policy":"recompute","kv_connector_extra_config":{"use_ascend_direct":true,"prefill":{"dp_size":1,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1},"decode":{"dp_size":4,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1}}}' \
  > /mnt/share/dsv4_nightly/pcp4dp1_pd/p_26151.log 2>&1

实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
实验目的: 方案C形态(#21口径复测)——P端挂dspark5配置(供MTP层metadata满足KV同构校验)+spec图模式(去enforce-eager)。
变量改动: 与091500实验(全同prompt口径)同服务实例, 仅压测口径改为#21标准50%prefix(10.3K共享前缀+10.3K随机独有, 每请求独有token全不同)。
预期效果: 与exp2/exp3v2等历史50%口径直接可比。
结果: 全档0失败; C1 343/366ms(对照exp2 760ms, 历史最优); TTFT<1s保持至C8(896ms); TPOT块17.9-27.8ms(真实约3.1-4.8ms/tok, 全档<10ms达成);
  实测命中率66.4%(P端/metrics全阶梯窗口差值, 高于50%构造因共享前缀token化占比略高); C64 563tok/s。
环境: .108单机对半1P1D(repro_dsv容器, 原作者镜像), proxy:5322→P:7100/D:9100。

[10-30更新] 压测口径升级为并发×4呼叫(reqs=conc×4, 见agents.md五节); ×4全阶梯原始数据见profiling/benchmark_raw_x4呼叫口径.txt; ×4口径命中率窗口实测进行中(接力exp4重起后补抓)。TPOT全档19-23ms稳定, 吞吐C64=413tok/s未饱和; TTFT随呼叫量排队线性走高(半机单组PCP4引擎)。

启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/bench_exp4_50.pycmd/bench_exp4_50.py打开原文件 ↗
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * 430
def mkprompt(idx):
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}编号{idx*7919}：" + (S * 200 + f"变体{idx}-") * 2 + f"结尾{idx}"
    while len(uniq) < 10300:
        uniq += f"填充{idx}" + S * 3
    return (SHARED + uniq[:10300])[:21400]
conc = int(sys.argv[1])
results = []
def one(idx):
    body = json.dumps({"model": "dsv", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": mkprompt(idx)}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://141.61.45.108:5322/v1/chat/completions", data=body,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        results.append((ttft, cks, time.time() - t0))
    except Exception:
        results.append((None, 0, time.time() - t0))
th = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(i,)) for i in range(conc)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
wall = time.time() - t0
ok = [r for r in results if r[0]]
if ok:
    ttfts = sorted(r[0] for r in ok)
    tpots = [(r[2]*1000 - r[0]) / max(r[1]-1, 1) for r in ok if r[1] > 1]
    ntok = sum(r[1] for r in results)
    print(f"conc={conc} ok={len(ok)}/{conc} ttft_avg={sum(ttfts)/len(ttfts):.0f}ms ttft_max={ttfts[-1]:.0f}ms tpot_avg={sum(tpots)/len(tpots):.1f}ms thru={ntok/wall:.0f}tok/s wall={wall:.1f}s")
else:
    print(f"conc={conc} ok=0/{conc}")
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/bench_exp4_50r.pycmd/bench_exp4_50r.py打开原文件 ↗
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"

    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729)
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]

    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        results.append((ttft, cks, time.time() - t0))

        results.append((None, 0, time.time() - t0))

import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * 430
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [10000]   # 全局唯一起点, 跨档/跨波不重复
def next_id():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        return _gid[0]
def mkprompt():
    idx = next_id()
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
conc = int(sys.argv[1])
results = []
def one():
    body = json.dumps({"model": "dsv", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": mkprompt()}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://141.61.45.108:5322/v1/chat/completions", data=body,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        results.append((ttft, cks, time.time() - t0))
    except Exception:
        results.append((None, 0, time.time() - t0))
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(conc)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
wall = time.time() - t0
ok = [r for r in results if r[0]]
if ok:
    ttfts = sorted(r[0] for r in ok)
    tpots = [(r[2]*1000 - r[0]) / max(r[1]-1, 1) for r in ok if r[1] > 1]
    ntok = sum(r[1] for r in results)
    print(f"conc={conc} ok={len(ok)}/{conc} ttft_avg={sum(ttfts)/len(ttfts):.0f}ms ttft_max={ttfts[-1]:.0f}ms tpot_avg={sum(tpots)/len(tpots):.1f}ms thru={ntok/wall:.0f}tok/s wall={wall:.1f}s")
else:
    print(f"conc={conc} ok=0/{conc}")

import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * 430
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [20000]
def next_id():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        return _gid[0]
def mkprompt():
    idx = next_id()
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
conc = int(sys.argv[1])
reqs = conc * 4
results = []
def one():
    body = json.dumps({"model": "dsv", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": mkprompt()}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://141.61.45.108:5322/v1/chat/completions", data=body,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        results.append((ttft, cks, time.time() - t0))
    except Exception:
        results.append((None, 0, time.time() - t0))
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(reqs)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
wall = time.time() - t0
ok = [r for r in results if r[0]]
if ok:
    ttfts = sorted(r[0] for r in ok)
    tpots = [(r[2]*1000 - r[0]) / max(r[1]-1, 1) for r in ok if r[1] > 1]
    ntok = sum(r[1] for r in results)
    print(f"conc={conc} reqs={reqs} ok={len(ok)}/{reqs} ttft_avg={sum(ttfts)/len(ttfts):.0f}ms ttft_max={ttfts[-1]:.0f}ms tpot_avg={sum(tpots)/len(tpots):.1f}ms thru={ntok/wall:.0f}tok/s wall={wall:.1f}s")
else:
    print(f"conc={conc} reqs={reqs} ok=0/{reqs}")

#!/bin/bash
# exp4 D端: 原版dspark5图模式配方(=d_bs16.sh), 换端口9000不变
unset https_proxy http_proxy
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
source "$SCRIPT_DIR/net_env.sh"
export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER=1 VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=3000
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT=204 HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=120 HCCL_BUFFSIZE=1024
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=CCU_SCHED VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096 VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS=1
export OMP_PROC_BIND=false OMP_NUM_THREADS=10 PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE=1
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES="0,1,2,3"
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 \
  --host 0.0.0.0 --port 9100 --served-model-name dsv \
  --data-parallel-size 4 --tensor-parallel-size 1 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 1024 --max-num-seqs 56 \
  --async-scheduling --block-size 16 \
  --no-enable-prefix-caching --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager \
  --speculative-config '{"num_speculative_tokens":5,"method":"dspark","enforce_eager":false}' \
  --compilation-config '{"cudagraph_mode":"FULL_DECODE_ONLY"}' \
  --kv-cache-dtype fp8 --attention_config.indexer_kv_dtype fp8 \
  --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 --tool-call-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --enable-auto-tool-choice --reasoning-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.95 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  --trust-remote-code \
  --additional-config '{"ascend_compilation_config":{"enable_npugraph_ex":true,"enable_static_kernel":false},"enable_cpu_binding":true,"multistream_overlap_shared_expert":true,"recompute_scheduler_enable":true,"enable_shared_expert_dp":true,"combine_quant_mode":4}' \
  --kv-transfer-config '{"kv_connector":"MooncakeConnectorV2","kv_role":"kv_consumer","kv_port":"30110","engine_id":"1","kv_load_failure_policy":"recompute","kv_connector_extra_config":{"prefill":{"dp_size":1,"tp_size":1},"decode":{"dp_size":4,"tp_size":1}}}' \
  2>&1 | tee v4_flash_d_exp4.log

#!/bin/bash
# exp4 P端: TP1xPCP4xDP1 + dspark5 + spec图模式(去enforce-eager) + prefix caching
unset https_proxy http_proxy
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
source "$SCRIPT_DIR/net_env.sh"
export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER=1 VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=3000
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT=204 HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT=120 HCCL_BUFFSIZE=1024
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=CCU_SCHED VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096 VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS=1
export OMP_PROC_BIND=false OMP_NUM_THREADS=10 PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE=1
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES="4,5,6,7"
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 \
  --host 0.0.0.0 --port 7100 --served-model-name dsv \
  --data-parallel-size 1 --tensor-parallel-size 1 --prefill-context-parallel-size 4 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --block-size 32 \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --enable-prefix-caching \
  --speculative-config '{"num_speculative_tokens":5,"method":"dspark","enforce_eager":false}' \
  --compilation-config '{"cudagraph_mode":"FULL_DECODE_ONLY"}' \
  --kv-cache-dtype fp8 --attention_config.indexer_kv_dtype fp8 \
  --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 --tool-call-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --enable-auto-tool-choice --reasoning-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  --trust-remote-code \
  --additional-config '{"enable_cpu_binding":true,"enable_fused_mc2":0,"multistream_overlap_shared_expert":true,"enable_shared_expert_dp":true}' \
  --kv-transfer-config '{"kv_connector":"MooncakeConnectorV2","kv_role":"kv_producer","kv_port":"30010","engine_id":"0","kv_load_failure_policy":"recompute","kv_connector_extra_config":{"prefill":{"dp_size":1,"tp_size":1},"decode":{"dp_size":4,"tp_size":1}}}' \
  2>&1 | tee v4_flash_p_exp4.log

# 与091500实验差异: 无配置改动, 仅压测口径改为#21标准50%prefix(10.3K共享+10.3K随机独有)

import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * 430
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [50000]
def next_id():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        return _gid[0]
def one():
    idx = next_id()
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    content = (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(sys.argv[1], data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        print(f"ttft={ttft:.0f}ms cks={cks}")
    except Exception as e:
        print("FAIL", str(e)[:80])
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(4)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
print(f"wall={time.time()-t0:.1f}s")

实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
# 单机对半 1P1D · prefix cache 50% 复测（×4 呼叫口径）

时间：2026-10-30 12:39 ｜ 环境：141.61.45.112（8×Ascend950DT，tp16n 容器，vllm-ascend v0.30.0-a5 20261021-r16）

## 背景与目的

94500 实验（同形态 1P1D，.108）按"50% prefix 构造"压测，实测命中率却达 66.4%（用户判：偏高）。
本轮目标：把《单机对半 1P1D · P=TP1×PCP4×DP1 + D=DP4×TP1 dspark5》的 prefix cache **实测**做到 50% 左右，并复跑完整 ×4 阶梯。

## 环境变迁（为何换到 .112）

- .111：本想复用，19:26/19:36 我方 P/D 进程被同一台机上另一位同事的 AI 代理（l30085666 的 vllm-ascend PR 验证会话 + dsv4.1 dp8 服务尝试）清理误杀，对方持续闪启占用风险高，放弃。
- .108：驱动级显存泄漏（kill 后 npu-smi 仍显 86GB/卡，reset 报 Please reboot OS），整机等重启。
- .112：扫段发现 8 卡全空闲且存在同名 tp16n 容器（镜像 sha256 与 .111 完全一致），迁移至此。

## 做了什么

1. exp4 原版脚本整套迁 .112（仅 sed IP），113s 全栈 ready（P:7100 / D:9100 / proxy:5322）。
2. 标定：三点实测命中率（mult250→20.4%、mult430→33.2%、mult665→67.8%，P 端 /metrics 窗口差值）。
3. **机理定案（66.4% 之谜）**：
   - `VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096` 使共享前缀块跨请求/跨档保活 → 暖态实测 = 构造的 **token 占比**（exp4 的 66.4% 即 m430 构造的 token 占比 2/3，并非构造失败）。
   - 命中存在**量子跳变**：每请求命中呈 8192/9216/12288 token 等离散台阶，随 m 非单调（m620→8192、m645→12288、m650→9216），纯共享长度无法精确落在 50%。
4. 解法（流量配比法）：共享构造取 m650，每 4 个请求里 1 个发纯随机（75/25），实测 **48.5%** 全档稳定——受 C1=4 请求整数约束，这是能诚实落地的最贴 50% 档。
5. 复跑 ×4 阶梯（1/4/8/16/32/64 并发 ×4 呼叫，输入 [:21400] 字符 ≈20K 字口径、输出 385，经 proxy:5322），**每档独立抓 P 端 /metrics 命中窗口**，全档 0 失败。

## 结果

| 档 | 命中率 | TTFT avg/max | TPOT(块) | 吞吐 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1(4) | 48.5% | 984/1,790 ms | 19.1 ms | 119 tok/s |
| C4(16) | 48.5% | 2,588/5,279 ms | 21.5 ms | 271 tok/s |
| C8(32) | 48.5% | 5,203/9,891 ms | 22.7 ms | 339 tok/s |
| C16(64) | 48.5% | 9,997/19,255 ms | 22.8 ms | 389 tok/s |
| C32(128) | 48.5% | 19,185/37,961 ms | 23.0 ms | 421 tok/s |
| C64(256) | 48.5% | 37,959/75,242 ms | 22.9 ms | 440 tok/s |

对照 66.4% 版（94500）：TPOT 持平（22.8→22.9ms），C64 吞吐 413→440 tok/s（+6.5%），TTFT 同量级（.112 与 .108 单机差异内）。

标定包络：44.6%（纯共享 m620）/ 48.5%（75/25 配比）/ 67.8%（m665）三档完整阶梯均归档。

- cmd/：P/D/proxy 启动脚本（exp4 原版 sed IP）、net_env.sh、bench_112_mix50.py（正式压测）、probe_cal.py（标定探针）
- profiling/：四份阶梯原始记录、CALIBRATION.md（全部标定点）、metrics_final.txt
- logs/：P/D 服务日志尾部 200 行

启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/bench_112_mix50.pycmd/bench_112_mix50.py打开原文件 ↗
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [40000]
def next_id():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        return _gid[0]
def mkprompt():
    idx = next_id()
    if idx % 4 == 0:
        rng0 = random.Random(idx * 7919 + int(time.time()))
        return "".join(rng0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
conc = int(sys.argv[1])
reqs = conc * 4
results = []
def one():
    body = json.dumps({"model": "dsv", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": mkprompt()}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://141.61.45.112:5322/v1/chat/completions", data=body,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        results.append((ttft, cks, time.time() - t0))
    except Exception:
        results.append((None, 0, time.time() - t0))
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(reqs)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
wall = time.time() - t0
ok = [r for r in results if r[0]]
if ok:
    ttfts = sorted(r[0] for r in ok)
    tpots = [(r[2]*1000 - r[0]) / max(r[1]-1, 1) for r in ok if r[1] > 1]
    ntok = sum(r[1] for r in results)
    print(f"conc={conc} reqs={reqs} ok={len(ok)}/{reqs} ttft_avg={sum(ttfts)/len(ttfts):.0f}ms ttft_max={ttfts[-1]:.0f}ms tpot_avg={sum(tpots)/len(tpots):.1f}ms thru={ntok/wall:.0f}tok/s wall={wall:.1f}s")
else:
    print(f"conc={conc} reqs={reqs} ok=0/{reqs}")

启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/net_env.shcmd/net_env.sh打开原文件 ↗
#!/bin/bash
# Auto-detect local NIC / IPv4 for P/D/proxy scripts.
# Optional overrides: NIC_NAME=xxx LOCAL_IP=yyy bash p.sh

_detect_nic() {
    if [ -n "${NIC_NAME:-}" ]; then
        echo "$NIC_NAME"
        return 0
    fi
    if [ -e /sys/class/net/enp34s0f1 ]; then
        echo "enp34s0f1"
        return 0
    fi
    local iface
    iface=$(awk '$2=="00000000" {print $1; exit}' /proc/net/route 2>/dev/null)
    if [ -n "$iface" ] && [ "$iface" != "lo" ]; then
        echo "$iface"
        return 0
    fi
    iface=$(ls /sys/class/net 2>/dev/null \
        | grep -vE '^(lo|docker|virbr|cali|tunl|endvnic|ipourma)' \
        | head -n1)
    [ -n "$iface" ] && echo "$iface"
}

_detect_ip() {
    local nic="$1"
    if [ -n "${LOCAL_IP:-}" ]; then
        echo "$LOCAL_IP"
        return 0
    fi
    local ip
    if command -v ifconfig >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        ip=$(ifconfig "$nic" 2>/dev/null | awk '/inet /{print $2; exit}' | sed 's/^addr://')
        [ -n "$ip" ] && { echo "$ip"; return 0; }
    fi
    if command -v ip >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        ip=$(ip -4 -o addr show dev "$nic" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $4; exit}' | cut -d/ -f1)
        [ -n "$ip" ] && { echo "$ip"; return 0; }
    fi
    if command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        python3 - "$nic" <<'PY'
import fcntl, socket, struct, sys
nic = sys.argv[1]
s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
try:
    print(socket.inet_ntoa(
        fcntl.ioctl(s.fileno(), 0x8915,
                    struct.pack('256s', nic.encode()[:15]))[20:24]))
except OSError:
    pass
PY
    fi
}

nic_name=$(_detect_nic)
if [ -z "$nic_name" ]; then
    echo "ERROR: cannot detect network interface; set NIC_NAME manually" >&2
    return 1 2>/dev/null || exit 1
fi
local_ip=$(_detect_ip "$nic_name")
if [ -z "$local_ip" ]; then
    echo "ERROR: cannot detect IPv4 for $nic_name; set LOCAL_IP manually" >&2
    return 1 2>/dev/null || exit 1
fi
export nic_name local_ip
echo "[net_env] nic=$nic_name ip=$local_ip"
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/p_exp4_112.shcmd/p_exp4_112.sh打开原文件 ↗
#!/bin/bash
# exp4 P端: TP1xPCP4xDP1 + dspark5 + spec图模式(去enforce-eager) + prefix caching
unset https_proxy http_proxy
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"
source "$SCRIPT_DIR/net_env.sh"
export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER=1 VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=3000

# 与 94500（.108 exp4）差异（仅改动项）

**栈参数零改动**：P/D/proxy 三脚本 = .108 exp4 原版逐字复制，仅 sed `141.61.45.108 → 141.61.45.112`（HCCL_IF_IP 经 net_env.sh 自动探测本机 IP，实际无需改；日志文件名加 _112 后缀）。唯一实验变量 = **prefix 构造/流量配比**。

## 压测构造（bench_112_mix50.py）

| 项 | 值 |
|---|---|
| 共享段 | `"共享材料寅：" + "白日依山尽…" × 650`（13,006 字符） |
| 独有段 | `独立请求{idx}：` + 10,280 随机字符（每请求全局唯一） |
| 截断 | `(共享+独有)[:21400]` 字符 |
| **流量配比** | **每 4 请求第 j%4==3 个发纯随机 21,400 字符（无共享）→ 75/25** |
| 目标 | 实测命中率 ≈50%（C1=4 请求整数约束下最贴近 50% 的配比） |
| 口径 | 并发×4 呼叫：1/4/8/16/32/64 并发 → 4/16/32/64/128/256 请求；输出 385；流式；经 proxy:5322 |

## 命中率计量（每档独立窗口）

- 每档压测前后各抓一次 P 端 `/metrics`（:7100），取 `vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total / queries_total` 窗口差值。
- 全档实测 48.5%（六档一致）。

## 与 66.4% 版差异机理（详见 profiling/CALIBRATION.md）

1. `VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096` 保活 → 暖态实测 = 构造 token 占比（66.4% = m430 的 token 占比，非异常）。
2. 命中量子跳变（8192/9216/12288），纯共享长度无法精确落在 50% → 用流量配比法补齐。

## 启动脚本清单

- p_exp4_112.sh：P 端 TP1×PCP4×DP1+dspark5 图模式+prefix 开，卡 4-7，:7100，kv_producer:30010
- d_exp4_112.sh：D 端 DP4×TP1+dspark5+FULL_DECODE_ONLY+prefix 关，卡 0-3，:9100，kv_consumer:30110
- proxy_exp4b_112.sh：load_balance_proxy_server_example_new.py :5322 → 7100/9100
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/probe_cal.pycmd/probe_cal.py打开原文件 ↗
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
MULT = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 430
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * MULT
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [70000]
def one():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        idx = _gid[0]
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    content = (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
    ntok = len(content)
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 385, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(sys.argv[1], data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        print(f"ttft={ttft:.0f}ms cks={cks} len={ntok}", flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("FAIL", str(e)[:80], flush=True)
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(4)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
print(f"wall={time.time()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

Prefix Cache：48.5%
TTFT：见各档
TPOT：见各档
吞吐（合计）：见各档
单卡吞吐：None
备注（口径与说明）：单副本4卡PCP4纯P(与P1实例并存于.21, 卡4-7专用); 环境同PCP8轮(p21ct privileged容器); 图模式+prefix on+retention4096+interleave1; 插曲: 收并存P2实例时worker残留锁卡4-7(89GB), 按npu-smi proc-mem逐卡PID清理后正常; 标定: 1/4构造小窗口44.5%但六档大窗口每档齐刷刷48.5%(同batch共享块互垫+块对齐后稳态), 50%口径=48.5%; miss基线稳态964~3129ms(0%实测); C1命中首请求274~296ms(对照miss 1020ms, -71%); 对照: 卡数减半效应显著—C1(4请求)961 vs PCP8xDP1 424(8卡) vs PCP4xDP2 578(8卡), 即同为PCP4, 8卡DP2比4卡DP1快40%; 六档TTFT线性涨(单副本4卡prefill吞吐天花板), C64单请求摊176ms; 六档0失败(256/256); TPOT无(1tok)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PCP4单机: rand=miss基线 mix50=1/4随机 mix50b=1/6随机; ladder=六档x4; max_tokens=1; 每档metrics窗口"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26121"
MODE = sys.argv[1]
WAVES = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 4
CONC = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 4
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    ratio = 6 if MODE == "mix50b" else 4
    if MODE == "rand" or j % ratio == ratio - 1:
        r0 = random.Random(60000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line and ttft is None:
                    ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        res.append(ttft)
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def stats(res):
    ok = sorted(x for x in res if x is not None)
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(ok)/len(ok):.0f} max={ok[-1]:.0f} min={ok[0]:.0f} n={len(ok)}"
def run_window(conc, per_worker):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc) for _ in range(per_worker)]
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per_worker)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    rate = 100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0
    return res, rate, wall
h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
print(f"MODE={MODE} WAVES={WAVES} CONC={CONC} base hit={h0} q={q0}", flush=True)
alltt = []
for w in range(WAVES):
    res, rate, wall = run_window(CONC, 1)
    alltt += res
    print(f"wave{w}: {stats(res)} hit_cum={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
print(f"HIT_WINDOW tokens_hit={dh:.0f} tokens_query={dq:.0f} rate={100.0*dh/dq if dq else 0:.1f}%", flush=True)
if MODE == "ladder":
    for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
        res, rate, wall = run_window(conc, 4)
        ok = sorted(x for x in res if x is not None)
        if ok:
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(ok)/len(ok):.0f} max={ok[-1]:.0f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("LADDER-DONE", flush=True)
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/p_pcp4.shcmd/p_pcp4.sh打开原文件 ↗
#!/bin/bash
# 纯P TP1xPCP4xDP1 卡4-7 图模式+prefix+retention (.21 p21ct :26121)

export OMP_PROC_BIND='false'

export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT='204'
export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'
export VLLM_ENGINE_READY_TIMEOUT_S='7200'
export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES='4,5,6,7'
export HCCL_IF_IP='141.61.33.21'
export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'

export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
export VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 --host 0.0.0.0 --port 26121 \
  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \
  --tensor-parallel-size 1 --prefill-context-parallel-size 4 --data-parallel-size 1 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 32768 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --trust-remote-code --block-size 32 \
  --enable-prefix-caching --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --compilation-config '{"cudagraph_mode":"FULL_DECODE_ONLY"}' \
  --kv-cache-dtype fp8 --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' \
  --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  > /mnt/share/dsv4_nightly/pcp4_single/p_pcp4_26121.log 2>&1

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PCP4xDP2: rand/mix50/mix50b标定 + ladder385六档x4(TTFT+TPOT+命中率)"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26141"
MODE = sys.argv[1]
MT = 385 if MODE == "ladder385" else 1
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if MODE == "rand" or j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(88000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": MT, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = last = cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if cks == 1: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
                    last = time.time()
        tpot = (last - t0 - ttft / 1000.0) / (cks - 1) * 1000 if cks > 1 else 0.0
        res.append((ttft, tpot))
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def stats(res):
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
    tps = f" tpot_avg={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f}" if tp else ""
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} n={len(ok)}{tps}"
def win(conc, per):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
print(f"MODE={MODE}", flush=True)
if MODE in ("rand", "mix50"):
    WAVES = 4
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    for w in range(WAVES):
        res, rate, wall = win(4, 1)
        print(f"wave{w}: {stats(res)} hit_cum={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        time.sleep(2)
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    print(f"HIT_WINDOW rate={100.0*dh/dq if dq else 0:.1f}%", flush=True)
elif MODE == "ladder385":
    w = []; win(4, 1)
    print("warmup done", flush=True)
    for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
        res, rate, wall = win(conc, 4)
        ok = [x for x in res if x]
        if ok:
            tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} tpot={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("LADDER385-DONE", flush=True)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""1tok六档: 复用26131, mix50构造(已标定49.5%), 每档metrics窗口"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26131"
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(66000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line and ttft is None:
                    ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        res.append(ttft)
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def win(conc, per):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
win(4, 1)
print("warmup done", flush=True)
for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
    res, rate, wall = win(conc, 4)
    ok = sorted(x for x in res if x is not None)
    if ok:
        print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(ok)/len(ok):.0f} max={ok[-1]:.0f} min={ok[0]:.0f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
    time.sleep(3)
print("L1T-DONE", flush=True)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PCP4xDP2: rand/mix50/mix50b标定 + ladder385六档x4(TTFT+TPOT+命中率)"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26131"
MODE = sys.argv[1]
MT = 385 if MODE == "ladder385" else 1
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if MODE == "rand" or j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(88000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": MT, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = last = cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if cks == 1: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
                    last = time.time()
        tpot = (last - t0 - ttft / 1000.0) / (cks - 1) * 1000 if cks > 1 else 0.0
        res.append((ttft, tpot))
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def stats(res):
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
    tps = f" tpot_avg={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f}" if tp else ""
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} n={len(ok)}{tps}"
def win(conc, per):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
print(f"MODE={MODE}", flush=True)
if MODE in ("rand", "mix50"):
    WAVES = 4
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    for w in range(WAVES):
        res, rate, wall = win(4, 1)
        print(f"wave{w}: {stats(res)} hit_cum={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        time.sleep(2)
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    print(f"HIT_WINDOW rate={100.0*dh/dq if dq else 0:.1f}%", flush=True)
elif MODE == "ladder385":
    w = []; win(4, 1)
    print("warmup done", flush=True)
    for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
        res, rate, wall = win(conc, 4)
        ok = [x for x in res if x]
        if ok:
            tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} tpot={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("LADDER385-DONE", flush=True)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""重测C1: 暖机4波(不计) -> C1x4波每波独立窗口"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26131"
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(55000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line and ttft is None:
                    ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        res.append(ttft)
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def win(conc):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
print("warmup x4 (不计窗口)...", flush=True)
for w in range(4):
    res, rate, wall = win(4)
    print(f" warm{w}: ttft_avg={sum(x for x in res if x)/max(1,len([x for x in res if x])):.0f} hit={rate:.1f}%", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
print("C1 x4 (每波独立窗口):", flush=True)
for w in range(4):
    res, rate, wall = win(1)
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    print(f" C1w{w}: ttft_avg={sum(ok)/max(1,len(ok)):.0f} hit={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
print("C4 x2:", flush=True)
for w in range(2):
    res, rate, wall = win(4)
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    print(f" C4w{w}: ttft_avg={sum(ok)/max(1,len(ok)):.0f} hit={rate:.1f}%", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
print("RE6-DONE", flush=True)

实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
# 单机8卡 纯P一体式 TP1×PCP4×DP2 · prefix 50% 实测（max_tokens=1）

时间：2026-10-30 15:01 ｜ 环境：141.61.45.112（8×Ascend950DT，tp16n 容器，v0.30.0-a5 20261021-r16）

## 目的

补齐 184000/235500 两条纯P实验缺失的 **prefix 实测命中率**（当时只写构造口径"未实测，环境已回收"）。

## 与归档 235500 的差异

- 迁机 .23→.112；prefix 开 + retention 4096
- **enforce-eager（235500 是去eager+FULL_DECODE_ONLY）**：本镜像（20261021-r16）上 PCP4×DP2+FULL_DECODE_ONLY 图捕获存在 bug——causal_conv1d_update 回退 PyTorch 导致捕获 stall，引擎加载完权重后被 watchdog 杀（无声死亡）。复现 3 次，改 eager 后稳定。图模式差异已记录。
- 显式 --data-parallel-rpc-port 19005

## DP2 标定（双 engine /metrics 求和）

| 构造 | 冷窗 | 暖窗 | 对照单机 DP1 暖态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| m430 | 22.1% | **44.3%** | 44.3% 一致 |
| m650 | 33.8% | **67.5%** | 67.8% 一致 |

- 冷窗 = 单机的 2/3：DP2 均分路由，4 请求→每 engine 2 个（1 writer + 1 follower）
- 暖态与单机同律 = 构造 token 占比（12288/8192 量子跳变同样存在）

## 正式结果（75/25 配比法，寅·m650）

| 波次 | 实测命中率 | TTFT（4 并发 20K，max_tokens=1） |
|---|---|---|
| 全 miss 对照 ×3 | 0% | 1003/2134 ms |
| 命中 ×4 | **48.5% 稳定** | 279–1521 ms（最快 279ms） |

首请求 TTFT：1043 → 282ms（**−73%**）。对照 184000（构造50%未实测，命中态 672–930ms）：本轮命中态 TTFT 更优且命中率可溯源。

## 运维发现（重要）

.112 在清理 1P1D 栈时发生**驱动级 HBM 泄漏**（进程全清后 8 卡仍钉 92GB）。解法（与 .108"必须整机重启"不同）：
`kill 宿主机孤儿进程` + `yes Y | npu-smi set -t reset -i <id>` 逐卡重置 → HBM 立即归还。已写回 agents.md 待更新。

主机: 141.61.45.112 (8x Ascend950DT 98GB)
备注: .112 曾发生驱动级HBM泄漏(杀进程后92GB不归还),
解法: kill宿主机孤儿 + yes Y | npu-smi set -t reset -i <id> 逐卡重置即回收(无需整机重启, 与.108结论不同)
另: 本镜像上 PCP4xDP2+FULL_DECODE_ONLY 图捕获有bug(causal_conv1d回退致捕获stall), 纯P用enforce-eager

启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/mix_probe1.pycmd/mix_probe1.py打开原文件 ↗
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
def mkprompt(j):
    if j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(70000 + j * 7919 + int(time.time()))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]

    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": mkprompt(j)}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request("http://141.61.45.112:26101/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                pass
        results.append((time.time()-t0)*1000)
    except Exception as e:
        results.append(None)

for t in th: t.join()

import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
MULT = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 430
SHARED = "共享材料甲：" + S * MULT
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = __import__("threading").Lock()
_gid = [70000]
def one():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1
        idx = _gid[0]
    rng = random.Random(idx * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{idx}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    content = (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
    ntok = len(content)
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(sys.argv[1], data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None; cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if ttft is None: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        print(f"ttft={ttft:.0f}ms cks={cks} len={ntok}", flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("FAIL", str(e)[:80], flush=True)
th = [threading.Thread(target=one) for _ in range(4)]
t0 = time.time()
for t in th: t.start()
for t in th: t.join()
print(f"wall={time.time()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"

    if True:
        r0 = random.Random(80000 + j * 7919 + int(time.time()))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + int(time.time()))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PCP4xDP2: rand/mix50/mix50b标定 + ladder385六档x4(TTFT+TPOT+命中率)"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26131"
MODE = sys.argv[1]
MT = 385 if MODE == "ladder385" else 1
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if MODE == "rand" or j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(88000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": MT, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = last = cks = 0
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                    cks += 1
                    if cks == 1: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
                    last = time.time()
        tpot = (last - t0 - ttft / 1000.0) / (cks - 1) * 1000 if cks > 1 else 0.0
        res.append((ttft, tpot))
    except Exception:
        res.append(None)
def stats(res):
    ok = [x for x in res if x]
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
    tps = f" tpot_avg={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f}" if tp else ""
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} n={len(ok)}{tps}"
def win(conc, per):
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(conc * per)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    return res, (100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0), wall
print(f"MODE={MODE}", flush=True)
if MODE in ("rand", "mix50"):
    WAVES = 4
    h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
    for w in range(WAVES):
        res, rate, wall = win(4, 1)
        print(f"wave{w}: {stats(res)} hit_cum={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        time.sleep(2)
    h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
    print(f"HIT_WINDOW rate={100.0*dh/dq if dq else 0:.1f}%", flush=True)
elif MODE == "ladder385":
    w = []; win(4, 1)
    print("warmup done", flush=True)
    for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
        res, rate, wall = win(conc, 4)
        ok = [x for x in res if x]
        if ok:
            tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} max={tt[-1]:.0f} tpot={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{conc*4} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"C{conc}({conc*4}) ALL-FAILED", flush=True)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("LADDER385-DONE", flush=True)

export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'

export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES='0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7'
export HCCL_IF_IP='141.61.33.21'
export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'

  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \

  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \

  --enable-prefix-caching --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --compilation-config '{"cudagraph_mode":"FULL_DECODE_ONLY"}' \
  --kv-cache-dtype fp8 --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' \

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""六档阶梯: C1/4/8/16/32/64, 请求=并发x4, mix50b构造, 每档独立metrics窗口"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26105"
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if j % 6 == 5:
        r0 = random.Random(97000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def worker(res, n_req):
    for _ in range(n_req):
        content = mkprompt()
        body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                           "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
        req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
        t0 = time.time(); ttft = None
        try:
            with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
                for line in r:
                    if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line and ttft is None:
                        ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
            res.append(ttft)
        except Exception:
            res.append(None)
print("warmup...", flush=True)
w = []
worker(w, 4)
h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
print(f"C1 warmup ttfts={sorted(x for x in w if x)} base hit={h0} q={q0}", flush=True)
for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
    n = conc * 4
    per = 4
    hh0, qq0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(res, per)) for _ in range(conc)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    hh1, qq1 = metrics_hit()
    ok = sorted(x for x in res if x is not None)
    dh, dq = (hh1 or 0) - (hh0 or 0), (qq1 or 0) - (qq0 or 0)
    rate = 100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0
    if ok:
        avg = sum(ok) / len(ok)
        print(f"C{conc}({n}) ttft_avg={avg:.0f} ttft_max={ok[-1]:.0f} ttft_min={ok[0]:.0f} hit={rate:.1f}% n_ok={len(ok)}/{n} wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"C{conc}({n}) ALL-FAILED wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    time.sleep(3)
print("LADDER-DONE", flush=True)
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/bench_ladder385.pycmd/bench_ladder385.py打开原文件 ↗
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""六档阶梯 385输出: TTFT + TPOT + 命中率窗口, mix50b构造, 请求=并发x4"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26105"
MT = 385
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if j % 6 == 5:
        r0 = random.Random(98000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=30) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def worker(res, n_req):
    for _ in range(n_req):
        content = mkprompt()
        body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                           "max_tokens": MT, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
        req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
        t0 = time.time(); ttft = last = cks = 0
        try:
            with op.open(req, timeout=600) as r:
                for line in r:
                    if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line:
                        cks += 1
                        if cks == 1: ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
                        last = time.time()
            if cks > 1:
                tpot = (last - t0 - ttft / 1000.0) / (cks - 1) * 1000
            else:
                tpot = 0.0
            res.append((ttft, tpot, cks))
        except Exception:
            res.append(None)
def stats(v):
    ok = [x for x in v if x]
    if not ok: return "ALL-FAILED"
    tt = sorted(x[0] for x in ok); tp = [x[1] for x in ok if x[1] > 0]
    return f"ttft_avg={sum(tt)/len(tt):.0f} ttft_max={tt[-1]:.0f} tpot_avg={sum(tp)/len(tp):.1f} n_ok={len(ok)}/{len(v)}"
print("warmup x1...", flush=True)
w = []; worker(w, 1)
print("warmup:", stats(w), flush=True)
for conc in [1, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]:
    n = conc * 4
    hh0, qq0 = metrics_hit()
    res = []
    ths = [threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(res, 4)) for _ in range(conc)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in ths: t.start()
    for t in ths: t.join()
    wall = time.time() - t0
    hh1, qq1 = metrics_hit()
    dh, dq = (hh1 or 0) - (hh0 or 0), (qq1 or 0) - (qq0 or 0)
    rate = 100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0
    print(f"C{conc}({n}) {stats(res)} hit={rate:.1f}% wall={wall:.1f}s", flush=True)
    time.sleep(3)
print("LADDER385-DONE", flush=True)
启动参数 / 脚本 cmd/bench_pcp8.pycmd/bench_pcp8.py打开原文件 ↗
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""PCP8纯P压测: rand=全随机(miss基线) mix50=75/25配比(≈50%口径); 每波4并发max_tokens=1; 窗口metrics算命中率"""
import json, time, urllib.request, threading, sys, random
URL = "http://127.0.0.1:26105"
MODE = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "mix50"
WAVES = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 4
S = "白日依山尽，黄河入海流。欲穷千里目，更上一层楼。"
SHARED = "共享材料寅：" + S * 650
CH = "甲乙丙丁戊己庚辛壬癸子丑寅卯辰巳午未申酉戌亥"
_lock = threading.Lock(); _gid = [0]
def mkprompt():
    with _lock:
        _gid[0] += 1; j = _gid[0]
    if MODE == "rand" or j % 4 == 3:
        r0 = random.Random(90000 + j * 7919 + (int(time.time() * 1000) % 1000000))
        return "".join(r0.choice(CH) for _ in range(21400))
    rng = random.Random(j * 104729 + (int(time.time()) % 100000))
    uniq = f"独立请求{j}：" + "".join(rng.choice(CH) for _ in range(10280))
    return (SHARED + uniq)[:21400]
def metrics_hit():
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    with op.open(URL + "/metrics", timeout=20) as r:
        txt = r.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    h = q = None
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total"):
            try: h = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
        if line.startswith("vllm:prefix_cache_queries_total"):
            try: q = float(line.split()[-1])
            except Exception: pass
    return h, q
def one(res):
    content = mkprompt()
    body = json.dumps({"model": "ds-flash-fp8", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                       "max_tokens": 1, "temperature": 0, "stream": True}).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL + "/v1/chat/completions", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    op = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
    t0 = time.time(); ttft = None
    try:
        with op.open(req, timeout=300) as r:
            for line in r:
                if line.startswith(b"data:") and b"[DONE]" not in line and ttft is None:
                    ttft = (time.time() - t0) * 1000
        res.append(ttft)
    except Exception as e:
        res.append(None); print("FAIL " + str(e)[:60], flush=True)
h0, q0 = metrics_hit()
print(f"MODE={MODE} WAVES={WAVES} metrics0 hit={h0} query={q0}", flush=True)
for w in range(WAVES):
    res = []
    th = [threading.Thread(target=one, args=(res,)) for _ in range(4)]
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in th: t.start()
    for t in th: t.join()
    ok = sorted([x for x in res if x is not None])
    print(f"wave{w}: ttfts={','.join(('%.0f' % x) for x in ok)} n={len(ok)}/4 wall={time.time()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
    time.sleep(2)
h1, q1 = metrics_hit()
dh, dq = (h1 or 0) - (h0 or 0), (q1 or 0) - (q0 or 0)
rate = 100.0 * dh / dq if dq else 0.0
print(f"HIT_WINDOW tokens_hit={dh:.0f} tokens_query={dq:.0f} rate={rate:.1f}%", flush=True)

#!/bin/bash
# 纯P TP1xPCP8xDP1(8卡) 图模式+prefix+retention (.21 dsv4_ray16_head, :26105)
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER='1'
export OMP_PROC_BIND='false'
export OMP_NUM_THREADS='10'
export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF='expandable_segments:True'
export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE='1'
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE='CCU_SCHED'
export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT='204'
export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'
export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES='0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7'
export HCCL_IF_IP='141.61.33.21'
export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME='lo'
unset http_proxy https_proxy ftp_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY FTP_PROXY
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
export VLLM_ENGINE_READY_TIMEOUT_S='7200'
export VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 --host 0.0.0.0 --port 26105 \
  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \
  --tensor-parallel-size 1 --prefill-context-parallel-size 8 --data-parallel-size 1 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 32768 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --trust-remote-code --block-size 32 \
  --enable-prefix-caching --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --compilation-config '{"cudagraph_mode":"FULL_DECODE_ONLY"}' \
  --kv-cache-dtype fp8 --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' \
  --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  > /mnt/share/dsv4_nightly/pcp8_single/p_pcp8_26105.log 2>&1

失败PD2机_21基线叠D端dspark_全recompute失效_20261030_0008002026-10-30 00:0800↑ 返回详情目录
镜像：vllm-ascend v0.30.0-a5 daily(dev166)
组网：双机 PD · D(.22)叠dspark5 · MooncakeV2
关键失败原因：D端报错"Mooncake producer metadata is missing layers [mtp.1.*]": dspark-D的KV含MTP层而P端无draft层, KV拉取100%失败→全recompute; 源码定位: pull_worker.py:310层覆盖校验为all-or-nothing; C1 6.9s/TPOT钉165ms
实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
实验目的: #21拓扑消融——D端叠dspark5(exp1)。
变量改动: 仅D=.22挂dspark5, 其余同#21基线。
预期效果: decode加速。
实际结果: [负向] C1~6.9s/165ms C16~17.2s/166ms C64~31.0s/166ms; KV转移0, 全部请求D端本地重算。
失败原因(第一条报错, 摘自当时D端日志): "Mooncake producer metadata is missing layers required by this worker: [mtp.1.self_attn...]"
  —— dspark-D的KV含MTP层, 要求P端metadata具备同构MTP层; P端无dspark则拉取100%失败, 触发kv_load_failure_policy=recompute全量重算。
修复路径见success/PD2机_双特性修复版(17节): P端同挂dspark5后KV转移8/8成功。
压测口径: 同上。

#!/bin/bash
# exp2/3 P端: TP1xPCP4xDP2+EP producer (P=.23 producer / D=.22 consumer, MooncakeConnectorV2, 治理补丁: recompute)
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER='1'
export OMP_PROC_BIND='false'
export OMP_NUM_THREADS='10'
export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF='expandable_segments:True'
export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE='1'
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE='CCU_SCHED'
export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'
export HCCL_EXEC_TIMEOUT='204'
export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'
export VLLM_EXECUTE_MODEL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS='3000'
export VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS='1'
export ASCEND_LOCAL_COMM_RES='{"version":"1.3"}'
export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES='0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7'
export HCCL_IF_IP='141.61.33.23'
export GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
export TP_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
export HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME='enp34s0f1'
unset http_proxy https_proxy ftp_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY FTP_PROXY
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$LD_LIBRARY_PATH:/usr/local/lib/
export VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL=4096
vllm serve /mnt/share/weight/DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731 --host 141.61.33.23 --port 26101 \
  --served-model-name ds-flash-fp8 \
  --data-parallel-size 2 --tensor-parallel-size 1 --pipeline-parallel-size 1 \
  --data-parallel-rpc-port 19000 --prefill-context-parallel-size 4 \
  --enable-expert-parallel --seed 1024 \
  --max-model-len 1048576 --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 --max-num-seqs 16 \
  --block-size 32 --enforce-eager \
  --no-disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager --enable-prefix-caching \
  --cp-kv-cache-interleave-size 1 \
  --model-loader-extra-config '{"enable_multithread_load":true,"num_threads":128}' \
  --trust-remote-code --kv-cache-dtype fp8 \
  --attention-config '{"indexer_kv_dtype": "fp8"}' --tokenizer-mode deepseek_v4 \
  --tool-call-parser deepseek_v4 --enable-auto-tool-choice --reasoning-parser deepseek_v4 \
  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \
  --additional-config '{"enable_cpu_binding":true,"enable_fused_mc2":0,"multistream_overlap_shared_expert":true,"enable_shared_expert_dp":true}' \
  --kv-transfer-config '{"kv_connector":"MooncakeConnectorV2","kv_role":"kv_producer","kv_port":"30000","engine_id":"0","kv_load_failure_policy":"recompute","kv_connector_extra_config":{"use_ascend_direct":true,"prefill":{"dp_size":2,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1},"decode":{"dp_size":4,"tp_size":1,"pp_size":1}}}' \
  > /mnt/share/.cache/.nx26/night_1029/p_pcp4_26101.log 2>&1 &
wait

第一条报错(时间2026-10-30 00:0x, D端.22 nx26容器日志):
WARNING/ERROR [MooncakeConnector] Mooncake producer metadata is missing layers required by this worker: ['mtp.1.self_attn.kv_a_proj_with_mqa', 'mtp.1.self_attn.kv_b_proj', ...] (MTP/draft层缺失)

#!/bin/bash
# exp2/3 P端: TP1xPCP4xDP2+EP producer (P=.23 producer / D=.22 consumer, MooncakeConnectorV2, 治理补丁: recompute)
export VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER='1'
export OMP_PROC_BIND='false'
export OMP_NUM_THREADS='10'
export PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF='expandable_segments:True'
export TASK_QUEUE_ENABLE='1'
export HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE='CCU_SCHED'
export HCCL_BUFFSIZE='1024'

export HCCL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT='120'

失败TP1xPCP4xDP2_纯P_显存被残留进程占用_20261029_1641402026-10-29 16:4140↑ 返回详情目录
镜像：vllm-ascend v0.30.0-a5 daily(dev166)
组网：单机8卡 纯P一体式 TP1×PCP4×DP2+EP
关键失败原因：NPU显存被上轮dspark残留worker占用, 无法分配显存启动失败; 清卡工具PID提取缺陷已修(改npu-smi proc-mem提取Process id)
实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗
实验目的: 同success纯P首字时延实验(首次点火)。
变量改动: 无。
实际结果: 点火失败, 显存被上轮dspark实验残留worker进程占用。
失败原因(第一条报错, 摘自当时日志): vllm启动时NPU显存不足(EngineCore无法分配/HCCL建域失败, 源于残留worker占卡)。
修复: 清卡工具PID提取缺陷修复(worker被setproctitle, pkill python3无效, 改用npu-smi info -t proc-mem提取Process id后kill -9), 重火成功(见success纯P首字时延实验)。
注: 原始日志已被后续成功轮覆盖, 本panic为当时诊断结论摘录。

实验简介exp_intro.txt打开原文件 ↗

结果: 启动READY(05:30:34)且首请求被接受, 但05:31:45 .112侧RayWorkerProc在gloo Pair::prepare处SIGABRT。
